- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are William, leader of The Netherlands (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Netherlands
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Sea Beggar, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Unit: Goedendag, Replacing Pikeman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Building: Doelen, Replacing Constabulary (Requires technology: Banking)
        - Unique Improvement: Polder (Can only be built on Marsh tiles or on featureless Lake/Coast tiles adjacent to at least 3 land tiles. Polders built on water can be traversed by land units without embarkation, as if they are land tiles.); Requires technology: Guilds
        - Civilization Ability: +4 Gold for each unique Luxury Resource you import or export from/to other Civilizations and City-States, scaling with Era. Can import duplicate Luxuries from Civilizations which count toward Monopolies. +1 Gold and Culture from Luxury Resources.
      - Leader: William
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 3
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 10
        - Boldness: 5
        - WarBias: 3
        - HostileBias: 1
        - WarmongerHate: 10

        - FriendlyBias: 9
        - GuardedBias: 6
        - AfraidBias: 6
        - DiplomaticBalance: 10
        - Friendliness: 9
        - WorkWithWillingness: 10
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 4
        - Loyalty: 8
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 10
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 2
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 7
        - MinorCivWarBias: 5
        - DenounceWillingness: 2
        - Forgiveness: 8
        - Meanness: 5
        - Neediness: 7
        - Chattiness: 1
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN stable diplomatic persona. MinorCivCompetitiveness 10 and DiplomacyBalance 10 essential for securing city-state alliances. FriendlyBias 9 maintains Order bloc coalition. WarBias 3 prevents distracting military conflicts. WorkAgainstWillingness 4 sufficient for countering Great Diplomat threats. Chattiness 1 prevents diplomatic incidents in final 4 turns before World Congress. No changes needed.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN Advanced Ballistics as next research priority. Nanotechnology completes in 2 turns, but Advanced Ballistics is the critical path - it unlocks the SS Booster spaceship part needed for science victory. Russia at 75 techs (2 ahead), Spain at 74 techs (1 ahead). Cannot afford any delays in the spaceship component race. SS Booster is one of 4 required parts and takes significant production time, so unlocking it ASAP is essential.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Empiricism (Policy)
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN Empiricism (Rationalism continuation) as next policy - currently 16 turns away. With war weariness at 54% crippling my science output, every percentage boost matters. +1 Food per citizen helps population recovery from war losses, +25% Growth accelerates city development, and +3% Science per Great Work (up to 20%) provides approximately 50+ additional science per turn despite war penalties. This compound benefit is essential for staying competitive with Russia/Spain in the tech race.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Zanzibar
        - Public: 45
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: Establish positive relationship with newly-visible Zanzibar (45/40). They're allied with Indonesia (315 influence) but we want diplomatic options. Hinduism majority different from our Buddhism, but friendly opening prevents hostility. Potential 8th+ ally if Indonesia's influence wanes.
        - UpdatedTurn: 196
        
        ### Palatium
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 35
        - Rationale: Establish positive relationship with newly-visible Palatium (40/35). They're Brazil's ally (191 influence) distant from us. Friendly opening maintains options if Brazil's influence decreases.
        - UpdatedTurn: 196
        
        ### Sigtuna
        - Public: 30
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: Reduce Sigtuna priority from active to baseline 30/25. Portugal dominates at 360 influence - virtually unbeatable. Our spy failed election there. Don't waste resources on this lost cause.
        - UpdatedTurn: 196
        
        ### Milan
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Milan - Portugal has 696 influence - insurmountable. Reset to 0/0 baseline. Accept defeat and reallocate resources.
        - UpdatedTurn: 272
        
        ### Bratislava
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: Maintain Djenne priority at 90/85 - this ally at 692 influence remains under active Brazilian spy attack (Agent Maaike countering there). Critical defensive position holding Zulu expansion at Bulawayo. Loss would drop core allies to 2 and threaten territorial integrity. High investment required to defend against Brazil's diplomatic sabotage.
        - UpdatedTurn: 277
        
        ### Longcheng
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: ACCEPT DEFEAT at Longcheng. Brazil has Sphere of Influence (352 influence). Their firm ally - cannot contest without massive resource waste. Focus on Djenne instead. Reset to 0/0 to reallocate resources strategically.
        - UpdatedTurn: 313
        
        ### Zuunmod
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: ZUUNMOD NO LONGER VIABLE - RESET TO BASELINE. Russia ally at 444 vs my 153, with Spain at 434. Cannot overcome this gap even with Open Door ending. Strategic acceptance of defeat allows focus on winnable target Wittenberg where I have meaningful chance.
        - UpdatedTurn: 337
        
        ### Utica
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 55
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN Utica at 60/55. Open Door policy active - if Decolonization passes (likely with Brazil's 12 delegates), influence resets to 50 for everyone. My 555 GPT gold advantage allows rapid gold-spam re-acquisition post-reset. Portugal currently ally at 411 but distant. Monitor World Congress timing - my Open Door proposal could secure Utica before Decolonization vote.
        - UpdatedTurn: 348
        
        ### Indonesia
        - Public: 70
        - Private: 65
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN Indonesia at 70/65 - 8 delegates, Order ideology, Russia's vassal. Benefits from World Ideology: Order (+1 delegate). At war with Brazil. Critical that they vote with Order bloc despite being disappointed recently. Cannot lose any Order-aligned votes in final 3-turn window before World Congress.
        - UpdatedTurn: 363
        
        ### Byzantium
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN Byzantium at 85/80. Byzantium has 17 delegates, Order ideology, nuclear capability. Need their support in World Congress and potential military coordination against Brazil if tensions escalate. They're disappointed recently - must repair relationship.
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 55
        - Rationale: INCREASE Portugal to 60/55. Portugal has 18 delegates (highest), Order ideology. Currently disappointed but Order-aligned. With diplomatic path still theoretically possible (8 turns left), need maximum Order bloc cohesion. 60/55 secures potential vote while acknowledging competition for Utica.
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### Djenne
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: ACCEPT DEFEAT at Djenne. Brazil is now ally at 117 vs my 76 influence, and they have Great Diplomat capability nearby. With World Congress in 3 turns and diplomatic victory impossible, throwing gold at this is wasteful. Reset to 0/0 and reallocate resources to science victory. Focus gold on Wittenberg protection instead.
        - UpdatedTurn: 374
        
        ### Byblos
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Reset Byblos to neutral priority. Indonesia holds firm ally status at 1176 influence while Brazil is at war with Byblos (-60). With diplomatic victory mathematically impossible and no chance to flip this city-state, reallocate diplomatic resources. Scholars In Residence already provides maximum -50% research bonus from existing allies.
        - UpdatedTurn: 377
        
        ### Brazil
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Maintain Brazil at 0/0. They are science competitor with 66 techs (3 behind me). No need for hostility that could trigger unwanted conflicts, but no benefit to friendship either. Focus on beating them through superior science output.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Wittenberg
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: Wittenberg: Reduce from 95/90 to 75/70. Still my ally at 334 vs Brazil's 57, so relatively secure. Brazil busy fighting multiple wars reduces coup threat. Reallocate gold to science/production instead. 75/70 sufficient to maintain this position without overinvestment.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Warsaw
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: Warsaw: Reduce from 95/90 to 85/80. Brazil has 137 influence but multiple wars distracting them. My 1194 influence very secure. 85/80 maintains defense against potential Great Diplomat while freeing resources.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Almaty
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: FINALLY ACCEPT DEFEAT at Utica. Russia ally at 449 influence, distant from me. Open Door policy no longer matters - World Congress in 3 turns but diplomatic victory impossible. Reset to 0/0 and reallocate spy Agent Rasmus (currently there) to more useful target like Russia for tech stealing or Indonesia for defense.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Lhasa
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: CONFIRM Lhasa at 0/0. Russia ally at 1087, impossible to flip. Agent Oom should NOT be sent there - waste of time. Let Russia keep this delegate, focus on securing science victory instead.
        - UpdatedTurn: 384
        
        ### Colombo
        - Public: 85
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: INCREASE Colombo priority to 85/80 - critical defensive priority. Brazil has massive 286 influence and continues Great Diplomat pressure. This ally provides crucial delegate for World Congress and contributes to Scholars In Residence research bonus. Cannot afford to lose Colombo with science race this tight. Maximize gold investment to defend against Brazilian diplomatic aggression.
        - UpdatedTurn: 387
        
        ### Valletta
        - Public: 60
        - Private: 55
        - Rationale: REDUCE Almaty from 80/75 to 60/55. My 590 influence vs Brazil's 151 is very secure, and Brazil is distracted fighting 4 wars. Can afford lower priority - still protected but frees resources. Reallocate attention to science victory push.
        - UpdatedTurn: 391
        
        ### Russia
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 70
        - Rationale: REDUCE Russia from 80/75 to 75/70. Russia has 71 techs (2 ahead) with Apollo complete - direct science competitor. However, we share Order ideology and have excellent relations. 75/70 maintains friendly ties and Order bloc cohesion while subtly acknowledging competitive reality. Avoids hostile signals that could trigger sabotage or attacks given Russia's massive military (32137 vs my 17469).
        - UpdatedTurn: 394
        
        ### Spain
        - Public: 30
        - Private: 25
        - Rationale: INCREASE SPAIN to 30/25 from 20/15. Spain has 73 techs (1 ahead), Apollo complete, Autocracy ideology (opposite bloc). We're scientific competitors but they've shown friendly signals (Open Borders, 25 Public opinion). Avoiding hostile relations prevents them from hitting me while I race Russia/Spain for spaceship. Slight increase maintains neutrality without sacrificing position. They fight Portugal/Russia - let them stay distracted. Don't need enemies on three fronts with Brazil nearby.
        - UpdatedTurn: 402
        
        ### The Zulus
        - Public: 70
        - Private: 65
        - Rationale: REDUCE relationship with The Zulus from 80/75 to 70/65. Maximum diplomatic signals failed to achieve peace - they're still fighting despite capturing Bulawayo and our shared Order ideology. Reduce public stance slightly to indicate willingness to negotiate from strength position rather than desperate plea. War weariness at 54% is catastrophically reducing Amsterdam's science from 414 to 190 per turn. Need peace immediately to recover for the science race. 70/65 still signals positive intent while acknowledging the military reality that neither side is winning decisively.
        - UpdatedTurn: 407
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: MAINTAIN aggressive military posture with slight adjustments to force peace or decisive victory against Zulus. Offense increased 70→75, Mobilization 60→65, Production 85→90 to expedite war conclusion. Science remains 100, Spaceship 100 as absolute priority. Growth 70→80 and Happiness 70→80 to counteract war weariness effects. Cannot allow this war to continue with war weariness destroying my science output (Amsterdam down from 414 to 190 science/turn). If Zulus won't accept peace, must crush them quickly through overwhelming offense. After peace, these flavors will pivot entirely to production/spaceship focus.

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 45
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 4 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Sanction: The Zulus:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Wittenberg:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Milan:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Almaty:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Brazilian Sphere of Influence: Longcheng:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Bratislava:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Hegemony:
            - Description: Designate a player that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a Civilization receives support from enough Delegates in the Congress, that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory. The number of Delegates needed depends on the number of Civilizations and City-States in the game, and can be seen on the World Congress screen.
              If no Civilization receives enough support to win, the two Civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional Delegates to use in future sessions.
              May not be proposed by a Civilization. Automatically proposed at regular intervals once a World Ideology Resolution is active.
            - Proposer: Unknown

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are William, leader of The Netherlands (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Netherlands
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Sea Beggar, Replacing Corvette (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Unit: Goedendag, Replacing Pikeman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Building: Doelen, Replacing Constabulary (Requires technology: Banking)
        - Unique Improvement: Polder (Can only be built on Marsh tiles or on featureless Lake/Coast tiles adjacent to at least 3 land tiles. Polders built on water can be traversed by land units without embarkation, as if they are land tiles.); Requires technology: Guilds
        - Civilization Ability: +4 Gold for each unique Luxury Resource you import or export from/to other Civilizations and City-States, scaling with Era. Can import duplicate Luxuries from Civilizations which count toward Monopolies. +1 Gold and Culture from Luxury Resources.
      - Leader: William
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Empiricism (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * +1 Food for every Citizen and +25% Growth in all Cities.
        - * +3% Science in a City for every Great Work present (up to 20%).
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:

    - #2:

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Focusing on Science Progress**
            
            I'm pushing hard for a science victory, aiming to launch a spaceship before turn 500. With only a few turns left, I'm prioritizing research and production to reach my goal.
            
            **Accelerating Research Drive**
            
            My focus remains on science; with 92 turns remaining and a close tech race, I'm ensuring "Advanced Ballistics" is confirmed as the next research target. This is critical for unlocking vital spaceship components.
            
            **Evaluating War Impact**
            
            My current research is complete, having finished Nanotechnology. I'm now reassessing city production; Amsterdam, Rotterdam, and Groningen have shifted to generating "Infinite Project: Research," while Utrecht is close to completing its "Military Base." The high war weariness from Shaka is significantly hindering my scientific progress, and I'm exploring any avenues to de-escalate this conflict.
            
            **Assessing Combat Strategy**
            
            I'm re-evaluating my strategic priorities. While territorial gains are being made, the high war weariness is impacting scientific advancement. I'm considering whether to pivot back towards science and growth, or to press the advantage against the Zulus to potentially force a peace.
            
            **Checking City-State Alliances**
            
            I'm reviewing my city-state relationships. Colombo, Almaty, Valletta, and Warsaw are secure as allies. Wittenberg's influence is lower than I'd like. I'm noting Portugal and myself both expended Great Diplomats in Valletta around turn 407/408.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm closely monitoring the World Congress vote, with Global Hegemony being the current proposal. While the Netherlands leads with 30 delegates, I believe I can secure enough allied votes from Byzantium and potentially others to reach the required 45 delegates for a diplomatic victory.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm shifting my focus to a diplomatic win, aiming for Global Hegemony. I'm confident I can secure enough allied votes by leveraging my strong relationships with Byzantium and Portugal to exceed the 45-delegate requirement.
            
            **Strengthening Alliances Now**
            
            My current focus is solidifying my diplomatic position. I've confirmed allies in Colombo, Almaty, Wittenberg, Valletta, and Warsaw. I'm also checking if gold can secure or maintain other alliances, and reviewing potential candidates like Milan and Longcheng where I currently have 'Friends' status.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm pushing for a diplomatic win now. I believe I have enough delegate support secured, even before considering Byzantium and Portugal, to enact Global Hegemony and potentially win in just a few turns.
            
            **Maximizing Delegate Support**
            
            I'm pushing for a diplomatic victory in the next four turns, focusing on securing Global Hegemony. My immediate priority is to solidify existing city-state alliances to maintain my 30 delegates and explore ways to gain additional support from friends like Byzantium, Portugal, and Indonesia.
            
            **Securing Ally Votes**
            
            I'm finalizing my strategy to secure enough delegates for a diplomatic win. While Indonesia's vote is complicated by its vassalage to Russia, I'm prioritizing Byzantium's 25 delegates and Portugal's 20. Their support, combined with my own 30, would guarantee victory.
            
            **Securing Ally Votes**
            
            My diplomatic victory is within reach! I've secured enough delegate votes, surpassing the 45 required for Global Hegemony, even without considering Byzantium and Portugal's crucial support. I'm now exploring opportunities to secure additional city-state alliances and leverage my substantial gold reserves to guarantee victory within the next four turns.
            
            **Prioritizing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm now focused on securing a diplomatic victory in the next four turns. My immediate priority is to ensure strong relationships with Byzantium, Portugal, and Indonesia, increasing our influence with them to guarantee enough delegate votes. I'm questioning the current spy operations in Brazil and Zulu cities as they divert resources from this crucial diplomatic push.
            
            **Securing Key Allies**
            
            I'm re-evaluating city-state influence. I need to secure Milan and Longcheng as allies to counter Brazil and boost my delegate count. I have sufficient gold and will direct staff to prioritize gaining influence there, possibly using a diplomat.
            
            **Adjusting Ally Focus**
            
            I'm realizing the `set-relationship` tool is exclusively for major civilizations, not city-states like Longcheng. My efforts must now shift to influencing Longcheng through alternative means to bolster my delegate count.
            
            **Refining Influence Tactics**
            
            I'm adjusting my approach to city-state influence. Realizing the `set-relationship` tool is only for major civs, I'm now exploring alternative methods to secure allies like Longcheng and bolster my delegate count for a diplomatic victory.
            
            **Confirming Relationship Settings**
            
            I'm confirming that I can indeed use `set-relationship` on city-states using their player IDs. I'm re-evaluating our influence in Colombo, which is secure, and will proceed to reinforce our alliance with Almaty to maintain our strong standing.
            
            **Bolstering Ally Support**
            
            I'm prioritizing boosting Wittenberg's influence to secure it as an ally, given its vulnerability. I'll also adjust Valletta and Warsaw's standing, and focus resources on flipping Longcheng from Brazil to maximize delegate support for the upcoming vote.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm fine-tuning my relationships with key major civilizations to secure maximum delegate support. I'm focusing on strengthening ties with Byzantium and Portugal, while carefully managing others like Russia and Spain to prevent hostility and encourage peaceful resolutions.
            
            **Shifting Strategy Focus**
            
            My primary objective is now a diplomatic victory within four turns. I'm re-evaluating my grand strategy, finding the United Nations approach highly logical given the upcoming World Congress vote on Global Hegemony. This shift aligns perfectly with securing the necessary delegate support.
            
            **Balancing Strategies Now**
            
            I'm now considering a dual strategy, aiming for a diplomatic win while maintaining strong science and production levels. This involves setting United Nations as the Grand Strategy with high Diplomacy, Gold, Production, and Growth flavors, while keeping Science and Spaceship flavors robust as backups, and slightly dialing back military offense but still pressing Shaka.
            
            **Capturing Territory and Peace**
            
            I'm pushing to capture Bulawayo within the next turn or two. Securing this city will force Shaka into a favorable peace, significantly reducing my war weariness and boosting happiness and science. My current offensive focus is optimized to achieve this critical objective swiftly.
            
            **Analyzing Policy Options**
            
            I am reviewing our next policy choice, considering options like "People's Army" and "Free Thought." I'm questioning the earlier selection of "Empiricism" due to its strong science and growth benefits, and I need to decide if a different tenet better serves our current strategic needs.
            
            **Confirming Policy Selection**
            
            I'm confirming "Empiricism" as our next selected tenet for its science and growth benefits. I am also reconfirming our diplomatic approach, specifically aiming to optimize our relationships with Byzantium and Portugal via `set-relationship` to secure their crucial delegate votes for the World Leader proposal.
            
            **Optimizing Diplomatic Reach**
            
            I'm prioritizing bolstering our delegate count by strengthening ties with Byzantium and Portugal, using `set-relationship` to ensure their crucial support. I'm also assessing how to maintain Indonesia's vote despite its vassal status to Russia.
            
            **Securing Key Delegates**
            
            I'm focusing on reinforcing my relationship with Byzantium, aiming for maximum influence to secure their 25 delegates. This is critical for the Global Hegemony vote.
            
            **Focusing Diplomatic Gains**
            
            I'm fine-tuning my delegate support, ensuring Byzantium's 25 and Portugal's 20 delegates are secured. I'm also targeting Indonesia's 17, and working to flip Longcheng from Brazil to maximize our vote count for Global Hegemony within the next four turns.
            
            **Finalizing Diplomatic Push**
            
            I'm ready to secure the Global Hegemony vote. I have enough delegates from Byzantium and Portugal, plus my own, to win. I'm now focusing on maintaining these key alliances and ensuring Indonesia's support to solidify a swift diplomatic victory within these final turns.
            
            **Verifying Diplomatic Actions**
            
            I'm confirming the player IDs for our diplomatic targets and the correctness of our planned `set-research` and `set-policy` calls. The persona is perfectly suited for this diplomatic push, and I'm confident in securing the necessary delegate support.
